| Lines | Says | Code | Mark | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 12:4-5 | Reference notes: Shop sells the base game Cardinals except the fourteen named ones (Moon to Bottom Rock), which are left out | `17_Cardinals.cs:65-70 NotSold; 17_Cardinals.cs:110-135 LoadStock` | MATCH | NotSold lists the fourteen names: Artifact_Moon, Mooncore, Loop, Loopedmirror, Vintage, Samedayshipping, Pig, Moneybag, Sandbox, Sand, Blurryphoto, Checklist, Computer, Bottomrock (68), plus Artifact_Rock (69, Bottom Rock's real id, pitfall #132). The Bottom Rock aura remark is a reason, not built. |
| 12:8 | What: a new Cardinal or Trinket is a run modifier, the same kind of effect as the base game's Cardinals | `17_Cardinals.cs:137-160 Build (Artifactobject and numbered upgrade copies); 17_Cardinals.cs:633-669 GrantCardinal; 24_Trinkets.cs:43-46` | MATCH | Each Cardinal is an Artifactobject whose Coreupgrades are put on the core, as the base game's are. |
| 12:9 | Names: found objects the wave washed up, two plain words each | `17_Cardinals.cs:44-62 Mine (Display fields)` | MATCH | All sixteen Display names are two words: Vampire Dentures, Brass Spyglass, Gutting Knife, Oil Can, Tar Bucket, Rubber Duck, Spare Plank, Punch Card, Pearl Oyster, Bottled Storm, Oilskin Coat, Gull Feather, Cork Lifebuoy, Signal Flare, Cargo Crate, Tin Whistle. |
| 12:10 | Bought from the shopkeeper with Pure Aura | `17_Cardinals.cs:548-579 Buy (p.Pure check and deduction); 18_Shop.cs:363-392 Buy; 18_Shop.cs:217-255 Shelf` | MATCH | Buy refuses with Not enough Pure Aura when p.Pure is below the price, then takes the price. |
| 12:11 | Two kinds, Cardinals and Trinkets, kept apart in the shop and in the list | `17_Cardinals.cs:215-219 IsTrinket; 17_Cardinals.cs:350-376 Shelf (TrinketSlots and OnShelf); 18_Shop.cs:234 card label` | MATCH | The shelf fills a Trinket slot separately from the Cardinal slots, and the shop card reads Trinket or Cardinal. |
| 12:12 | Trinket belongs to its buyer and acts only on that player's vessel, towers, units or aura, and shows on the vessel panel | `17_Cardinals.cs:883-901 Grant (panel upgrade on vessel); 17_Cardinals.cs:976-999 Reach (units and towers by owner); 24_Trinkets.cs:678-691 Pay (aura to the buyer)` | MATCH | Trinkets sit in RWPlayer.Trinkets (14_Run.cs:14-15) and are reached by owner filter. |
| 12:13 | Cardinal acts on the core, the enemies or the clock, is everyone's, shows in the Cardinal row, and works for everyone once one player buys it | `17_Cardinals.cs:633-669 GrantCardinal (every core, lobby Artifacts list for the row); 17_Cardinals.cs:1061-1099 Row (HUD row on every machine); 14_Run.cs:145-149 RWRun.Cardinals` | MATCH | Cardinals are added to RWRun.Cardinals (17_Cardinals.cs:568) and to every core (17_Cardinals.cs:653-656). |
| 12:14 | Base game split: the seventeen named stock Cardinals are sold as Trinkets, the other stock ones stay Cardinals | `17_Cardinals.cs:87-95 stockTable and StockTrinketIds (17 rows); 17_Cardinals.cs:221-226 StockKind; 17_Cardinals.cs:161 Sold adds the stock list` | MATCH | The seventeen ids are Beastpelt, Bluecoin, Caffeine, Diorama, Gavel, Greencoin, Paper, Redcoin, Rustypipe, Slippers, Titan, Voice, Yellowcoin, Badge, Bird, Kippy, Fishbait, which is the scope's set with Sport Water as Caffeine and Beast Tassle as Beastpelt. The ~12~ remaining stock ones are a runtime count, not checked here. |
| 12:15 | Base game Trinkets' rules: a Coin pays its buyer a share split by the players; Aura Gavel fires for the buyer's own units; Torrid Badge, Bendigo, Kippy and Fish Bait make their unit beside the buyer's vessel, one at a time | `24_Trinkets.cs:678-691 Pay (divides by players); 24_Trinkets.cs:511-512 Tide and 658-674 CoinStart; 24_Trinkets.cs:727-749 Gavel (own units); 24_Trinkets.cs:696-724 Companions and Despawn; 17_Cardinals.cs:905-923 GrantStock (Unit)` | MATCH | Companions keeps one unit per holder by despawning orphaned ones only once the new spawner stands (24:704-714). |
| 12:16 | Lobby picks work for everyone, even a stock Trinket, and the shop then stops selling that one | `17_Cardinals.cs:535-539 InLobby; 17_Cardinals.cs:322 StockTaken; 17_Cardinals.cs:400-407 MaxedFor; 17_Cardinals.cs:567 Buy refusal; 17_Cardinals.cs:822-869 LockLobby` | MATCH | A stock Trinket in the lobby is StockTaken, so it is never on the shelf (MaxedFor, 17:405). |
| 12:17 | No limit on how many Cardinals or Trinkets a campaign holds; past the Cardinal row's 8 the rest show as +N | `17_Cardinals.cs:605-619 Overflow; 17_Cardinals.cs:664 row cap of 8; 14_Run.cs:539 run line '+N Cardinals'` | MATCH | RWRun.Cardinals and Trinkets have no size limit; the row cap of 8 counts lobby picks. |
| 12:18 | Stacking: a stacking one works for up to 6 copies and leaves the shop at 6; a base Cardinal is bought once; a base Trinket is bought once by each player | `17_Cardinals.cs:32 Stacks = 6; 17_Cardinals.cs:337-341 Cap (Stacks, else 1); 17_Cardinals.cs:400-407 MaxedFor; 17_Cardinals.cs:561 Trinket cap` | MATCH | Stock ids have Cap 1, so each player holds one; the stock Trinket is capped per player (Trinkets list), not per run. |

RampFor uses RWRun.Cardinals (lobby excluded) and p.Trinkets.Count. |

## 3. Design pages against code

Each vessel's `DESIGN.md` (lines as D:N), every number and rule mapped to its code. The scope wins over a design page: a DIFFERENT row is fixed in whichever of the two the scope agrees with least.

| File | MATCH | DIFFERENT | NOT-IN-CODE |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Argo/DESIGN.md | 69 | 3 | 0 |
| Spool/DESIGN.md | 137 | 2 | 2 |
| Uloe/DESIGN.md | 116 | 1 | 3 |
| Yossele/DESIGN.md | 103 | 0 | 0 |

### 3.1 DIFFERENT and NOT-IN-CODE (to confirm by hand)

| File | Lines | Says | Code | Mark | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Argo/DESIGN.md | D:51 | AIM picker within 5 tiles places the Tremor | `16_AimSelect.cs:57-62; 07_TowerBrain.cs:761-780; 02_Data.cs:174` | DIFFERENT | Design: 5 tiles. Code: the picker reach is Range+0.5 = 5.5 (default 5.5 when unsynced), and the server refuses past Range+AimSlack = 5.5. |
| Argo/DESIGN.md | D:100-101 | Fallback if the fifth fails: Deep Water becomes the Drill Rig's FLOOD command | `06_HeroBrain.cs:96-113` | DIFFERENT | Design: Deep Water takes the Drill Rig's FLOOD. Code: a missing fifth option leaves four buttons and logs a warning; FLOOD stays on Deep Water's own Buildoption1. |
| Argo/DESIGN.md | D:156-157 | Kit 1.6: ModKitPack.Join, ModKitTides, ModKitCounter, ModKitFocus come in with Argo | `00_ArgoMod.cs:20-21,40,129; 05_Server.cs:41-48; 07_TowerBrain.cs:885` | DIFFERENT | Design says Kit 1.6. The code header files these four under Kit 1.7 (00_ArgoMod.cs:20-21); 05_Server.cs:41 says Kit 1.6 for ModKitTides. |
| Spool/DESIGN.md | D:37 | Health regen 0 | `Spool/Code/02_Data.cs:89-92` | NOT-IN-CODE | Searched HeroStats and BuildHero for a regen stat; none is set, so the template's value is inherited. |
| Spool/DESIGN.md | D:91 | No upgrades built yet on the Yarn Stash | `Spool/Code/02_Data.cs:294-310` | DIFFERENT | Code: the Yarn Stash has seven upgrades (Pockets, Quick, Darning, Elbow, Remnant, Circle, Bee); the page says none are built. |
| Spool/DESIGN.md | D:180 | Made of Tougher Stuff built by the mode | `Spool/Code/ (all files given)` | NOT-IN-CODE | Searched all Spool files for Tougher Stuff; only the mode file, not provided, would hold it. |
